# Test source

```ts
  44  | // ============================================
  45  | // Projects Page Component
  46  | // ============================================
  47  | 
  48  | export class ProjectsPage extends UiBase {
  49  |   constructor(options: TestContextOptions) {
  50  |     super(options);
  51  |   }
  52  | 
  53  |   // ============================================
  54  |   // Navigation (Public)
  55  |   // ============================================
  56  | 
  57  |   /**
  58  |    * Navigate to the Projects index. Call before any index-reading ATC.
  59  |    */
  60  |   @step
  61  |   async open(): Promise<void> {
  62  |     await this.page.goto(this.buildUrl('/projects'));
  63  |   }
  64  | 
  65  |   // ============================================
  66  |   // Helpers (Private)
  67  |   // ============================================
  68  | 
  69  |   private listItem(slug: string) {
  70  |     return this.page.locator(`[data-testid="projects-list-item-${slug}"]`);
  71  |   }
  72  | 
  73  |   // ============================================
  74  |   // ATCs - Complete Test Cases
  75  |   // ============================================
  76  | 
  77  |   /**
  78  |    * ATC: A workspace with projects lists them all, oldest first, with name
  79  |    * and slug — and the create form is not what greets the member.
  80  |    *
  81  |    * Staging is a SHARED workspace that already carries projects from other
  82  |    * test runs, so this asserts the seeded projects' relative order and
  83  |    * content among however many entries exist — not the list's total count
  84  |    * (that would make the ATC flaky against a workspace this session doesn't
  85  |    * own exclusively).
  86  |    *
  87  |    * @param expected - the seeded projects, already in oldest-first order
  88  |    */
  89  |   @atc('BK-604')
  90  |   async listProjectsOldestFirstWithNameAndSlug(expected: ProjectListEntry[]): Promise<void> {
  91  |     const list = this.page.locator('[data-testid="projects-list"]');
  92  |     await expect(list).toBeVisible();
  93  | 
  94  |     const items = list.locator('li');
  95  |     const testIds = await items.evaluateAll(nodes =>
  96  |       nodes.map(node => node.querySelector('[data-testid^="projects-list-item-"]')?.getAttribute('data-testid') ?? null));
  97  | 
  98  |     let previousIndex = -1;
  99  |     for (const project of expected) {
  100 |       const item = this.listItem(project.slug);
  101 |       await expect(item).toContainText(project.name);
  102 |       await expect(item).toContainText(project.slug);
  103 | 
  104 |       const resolvedIndex = testIds.indexOf(`projects-list-item-${project.slug}`);
  105 |       expect(resolvedIndex).toBeGreaterThan(previousIndex);
  106 |       previousIndex = resolvedIndex;
  107 |     }
  108 | 
  109 |     await expect(this.page.locator('[data-testid="create-project-form"]')).toHaveCount(0);
  110 |   }
  111 | 
  112 |   /**
  113 |    * ATC: a freshly seeded project renders correctly on the index — name,
  114 |    * slug, no empty/bare-form state around it.
  115 |    *
  116 |    * NOTE — BVA scope: BK-605's Jira TC is titled around the "exactly one
  117 |    * project" lower boundary, but staging is a shared workspace that already
  118 |    * carries projects seeded by other sessions/runs (never truly empty), so
  119 |    * the literal zero-then-one boundary cannot be exercised here — the same
  120 |    * class of environment gap already documented for BK-266's TC05/10/11
  121 |    * (`.context/PBI/.../acceptance-test-results.md`). This ATC instead
  122 |    * regression-guards that a single newly added project is never lost or
  123 |    * misrendered among however many others already exist.
  124 |    *
  125 |    * @param expected - the single freshly seeded project
  126 |    */
  127 |   @atc('BK-605')
  128 |   async listSingleProjectAtLowerBoundary(expected: ProjectListEntry): Promise<void> {
  129 |     const list = this.page.locator('[data-testid="projects-list"]');
  130 |     await expect(list).toBeVisible();
  131 | 
  132 |     const item = this.listItem(expected.slug);
  133 |     await expect(item).toContainText(expected.name);
  134 |     await expect(item).toContainText(expected.slug);
  135 |   }
  136 | 
  137 |   /**
  138 |    * ATC: Activating an index entry navigates to that exact project.
  139 |    *
  140 |    * @param slug - slug of the project entry to activate
  141 |    */
  142 |   @atc('BK-606')
  143 |   async activateProjectEntryNavigatesToProject(slug: string): Promise<void> {
> 144 |     await this.listItem(slug).click();
      |                               ^ TimeoutError: locator.click: Timeout 15000ms exceeded.
  145 |     await this.page.waitForURL(`**/projects/${slug}`);
  146 |     await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/projects/${slug}$`));
  147 |   }
  148 | 
  149 |   /**
  150 |    * ATC: A project's description shows when the author wrote one, and is
  151 |    * cleanly omitted (no empty line) when absent. Scenario Outline (BK-607) —
  152 |    * call once per row; both rows share this same `@atc` id.
  153 |    *
  154 |    * @param row - one Examples row: the project's slug and its description (or null)
```
